### Final qualification (FQ, stack N=29)

Stream `final` on `ws/conn-values` after merging the integration branch (`claude/parallel-agents-plan-uzpzm0`) in clio-agent, gact-tui, the marketplace and clio-kit. Stack N=29 (agent 8829, UI 5229), `CLIO_CLOUD_ISOLATE=1`, `CLIO_ARC_CTE_FILE_CAPACITY=128MB`, CLIO brand (logo read in the first shot); clio-kit and clio-schemas editable, paraview-mcp 3.1.0 re-installed editable (it had dropped out of the venv). Three configurations of the one stack, each from an emptied `.cloud-run/xdg`: (1) the first-run journeys and the offer card, nothing enabled; (2) the same config restarted with `connector-fixtures.env.sh` for AQ1 to AQ12, AQ38, comments and permissions (transcripts kept in `.cloud-run/fq-parta/`); (3) a fresh config for the website shots (ParaView enabled with "Command to run first" `export PATH=/opt/pv6/bin:$PATH`, workspace `wave-study` with a 72³, 12-step version of the wave fixture). Cluster: clio-kit `cluster.sh up` with `CLIO_PV_CLUSTER_PREFIX=clio-pv-fq`, ports 21922/21923, subnet 10.208.29, `CLIO_PV_CLUSTER_STATE=~/.ssh/clio-pv-fq`. #### Fixes made

| ID | Fix | Where | Tests | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| FQ-F1 | **Sign in and out at a connector's address in use.** The saved connection is a template (CV-D2), and the sign-in, sign-out, probe and delete routes keyed the token by its raw `url`, `${need.address}`; the connector's own check looks under the filled address, so a sign-in that connected left it "needs: account" (FQ-AQ3) and a revoked connector had no last listing to join a turn with (FQ-AQ6). `materialize.in_use` fills a connector's template from the defaults for those four routes | clio-agent `6f59b25c1` (`connectors/materialize.py`, `gact/mcp_auth.py`, `gact/routes/mcp_auth.py`, `gact/routes/mcp_configuration.py`) | `test_conversation_values.py::test_a_sign_in_is_stored_under_the_address_in_use`; test_connectors 92 passed | passed (live) |
| FQ-F2 | **A view opened on a file pattern is named by its folder**: JR2's `open_view(~/data/pulse_series/*)` tab read "ParaView: *" | clio-agent `3290a51b7` (`gact/mcp_app_label.py`) | `test_mcp_app_label.py::test_a_pattern_names_its_folder_or_its_own_stem` | tests only |
| FQ-F3 | **The offer card stays inside a phone-width card**: in a narrow card the options are a wrapping column whose line took the contents row's width, widening every button past the card | gact-tui `77303a514` | measured live (`ask-options` 312 px, children 312 px) | passed (live) |
| FQ-F4 | Harness: `journey.mjs` answers only the card the dock shows (two waiting cards are paged), reads "This session", waits while an answered offer turns its connector on, and can follow a running turn; `connectors_api.sh` and `connector_card.mjs` answer the agent's offer with Add, since enabling adds a connector to no conversation | clio-agent `85d2afcdd`, `7d48a06af`, `6f59b25c1`, `d53349b10` | — | done |

#### Decisions (the offer card, the person's request)

| ID | Decision | Where | Evidence | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| FQ-D1 | **`offer_plugin` requires `reason`** (no default, `minLength: 1`): a call without it is the typed argument error the model corrects, never a card with no why (CF-N4). Generally, a native tool call that leaves out an argument with no default is now `missing required argument …` (ToolArgumentError), not a TypeError inside the tool. The plugin results carry `description`; the row shows "What it is". Contracts *Amended* | clio-agent `6e7d18609` (`gact/plugin_offer.py`, `gact/plugin_offer_presentation.py`, `gact/agents/dspy_native_tool.py`) | `test_plugin_offer.py::test_the_reason_is_required_by_the_schema`, row test; 72 passed with the native-tool suites; every live offer after the restart carried a reason | passed (live) |
| FQ-D2 | **Four rows.** (1) the logo or puzzle icon, "Add <name> to this conversation?" with the plugin's readable name (the connector's name, else the id read as words: "Tariff calc"; the plugin schema has no display name) and the state at the right; (2) what it is: the plugin's own description (a connector's own description, not its guidance plugin's), one line, the whole on click; (3) "Why" and the agent's reason, one line, the whole on click; (4) Add, Not now (numbered keys first), then what Add brings in words, then View plugin and Settings (n). Required needs still show when there are some. No middle dots | gact-tui `60b2550ba`, `77303a514` (`connector-offer-response.tsx`, `connector-setup-activity.tsx`) | `connector-offer.test.tsx` (9), `plugin-offer-contents.test.ts` (4) | passed (live) |
| FQ-D3 | **What it brings, from the plugin row `GET /v1/plugins` already lists** for `plugin_ref` (a connector: its row's `plugin_ref` and `tool_count`), no new endpoint or payload field: non-zero kinds with the explorer's nouns, "Brings 1 skill, 1 MCP server and 2 prompt fragments to this conversation"; a connector is one MCP server with its tool count when listed (not counted twice when its plugin declares it); nothing known: "Adds it to this conversation only". A plugin's own MCP server's tools are unknown until it starts, so it reads "1 MCP server" | gact-tui `web/src/lib/plugin-offer-contents.ts` | unit tests; live rows recorded by `connector_offer.mjs` | passed (live) |
| FQ-D4 | **View plugin opens the existing plugin explorer** (`PluginExplorer`, the dialog the agent tab uses) over the conversation, rendered outside the card with its keys stopped there, so Escape closes the explorer and never answers the offer. Shown only when the catalog lists the plugin (a connector that is off and has only a carrier has none) | gact-tui `connector-offer-response.tsx` | vitest (dialog, Escape, card kept, not answered); live desktop and phone | passed (live) |

### More connectors (MC, stack N=30)

The test of generality: connectors CLIO had never seen, added as data only. The headline is a 3D globe (the MCP Apps map example) used together with NASA Earthdata from one plain request; a PDF viewer is the second MCP App. Stack N=30 (agent 8830, UI 5230) on `ws/conn-values`, fresh `CLIO_CLOUD_ISOLATE=1` configuration, `CLIO_ARC_CTE_FILE_CAPACITY=128MB`, the CLIO brand, clio-kit (paraview-mcp 3.1.0) and clio-schemas installed editable. The integration branch was merged twice: before the first runs (`4ca668a0e`) and in the middle (`98472df0d`, develop's web-search and literature built-ins and the gact-tui translations), after which the stack was restarted on a new clio-core store; rows say which code they ran on. Every request is plain unless the row says otherwise; permission prompts were answered "This session" by the harness (`allowPending`). Step files: `journey_enable.mjs`, `xapp_dashboard.mjs` (`XD_SESSION`), `connector_plugin_change.mjs`, `connector_offer.mjs`, `xapp_matrix.mjs` (gains `XM_OPEN_IN_SESSION` and a Reopen line found by scrolling a long transcript), `more_conn_answer.mjs` (new: answers a question or offer card as a person). Screenshots, all read, are in `/home/user/wt/gallery/more-conn/` (`mc<step>-…`, `mc-globe-…`); the website copies are `/home/user/wt/gallery/website/globe-{light,dark}-desktop.png` and `globe-dashboard-{light,dark}-desktop.png`.

#### The connectors and why

| ID | Connector | Source, pinned | Why it serves CLIO's users | What shipping takes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| MC-C0 | **3D globe** (MCP App, `ui://cesium-map/mcp-app.html`), the headline | `npx -y --silent @modelcontextprotocol/server-map@2.0.4 --stdio` (npm, 2026-10-27; source `modelcontextprotocol/ext-apps` `examples/map-server`, read at `ea3e9d0`). License MIT (moving to Apache-2.0). Entry `catalogs/connectors/globe.yaml` | Geoscience is CLIO's home ground (EarthScope GNSS, NDP discovery, Earthdata): a region, a station network's area or a dataset's search box on a real 3D globe, next to the data. With NASA Earthdata in the same conversation the agent searches and then shows where the search covers, from one plain request. It reports the camera to the agent and registers three app tools (`navigate-to`, `get-current-view`, `get-screenshot`) | **Opt-in, and only with MC-D1 and MC-F4.** Hosts reached: `cesium.com` (CesiumJS 1.123 from Cesium's CDN, Apache-2.0), `tile.openstreetmap.org` (tiles, ODbL data, attribution "© OpenStreetMap contributors" which the view shows; the OSMF tile usage policy forbids heavy use and asks for a valid User-Agent, so a shipped default should use a tile provider CLIO has terms with), `nominatim.openstreetmap.org` (geocoding, at most 1 request a second by policy; the server throttles to 1.1 s and sends its own User-Agent). No Cesium ion token is used (the ion logo shows because CesiumJS draws it). All three were reachable here. Limits: it draws a box and a label, not points or GeoJSON, so GNSS stations or swath footprints cannot be plotted (MC-U3); it is an example server. Maintenance as for the PDF viewer |
| MC-C1 | **PDF viewer** (MCP App, `ui://pdf-viewer/mcp-app.html`) | `npx -y --silent @modelcontextprotocol/server-pdf@2.0.4 --stdio` (npm, published 2026-10-27; source `modelcontextprotocol/ext-apps` `examples/pdf-server`, read at `ea3e9d0`, which is 2.1.0 unpublished). License MIT (the repository is moving to Apache-2.0). Entry `catalogs/connectors/pdf.yaml` | Scientists read and cite papers next to their analysis: the GRACE/MAGIC paper beside GNSS or water-storage data, an HPC code's method paper beside its output. The viewer reports the page the person is on, so the agent answers about what they read, can navigate, search, highlight and take page pictures (`interact`), and the view sits in a dashboard beside CLIO's own tables. It complements develop's `literature_search` (which finds papers and their readable address) and the native `view_pdf` (which reads a PDF into the model): this one is the person's reading surface | **Opt-in, not default, until MC-F3 is fixed** (every open stores the whole file in the conversation). Upstream is the MCP Apps reference example, maintained by the specification's own team and released with the SDK (healthy, but an example: its allow-list of archives and its 512 KB chunking are its own choices). Node.js 20+ on the machine (npx). No local files until CLIO advertises MCP roots (MC-O3). Maintenance: re-pin with each SDK release and re-run the matrix |
| MC-C2 | **NASA Earthdata** (plain, no view) | `uvx earthdata-mcp-server==0.6.0 start --transport stdio` (PyPI, 2026-10-10; `datalayer/earthdata-mcp-server`). License BSD-3-Clause. Entry `catalogs/connectors/earthdata.yaml` | Data discovery is CLIO's core (NDP, EarthScope GNSS); NASA's Common Metadata Repository is where satellite geodesy and hydrology data live (GRACE/GRACE-FO, SWOT, sea level). Searching datasets and their files is anonymous; downloads use a free Earthdata Login. Different shape from the catalog's others: a command server with an optional value need and an optional **secret** need, both in `env` | **Opt-in** (it installs earthaccess and s3fs through uvx, about 250 MB in CLIO's uv cache). Upstream is a company-maintained project (Datalayer) with regular releases; three tools; CMR is a stable public API. The download path was not run here (no Earthdata account in this session) |
| MC-X1 | arXiv (dropped) | `uvx arxiv-mcp-server==0.8.2` (`blazickjp/arxiv-mcp-server`, Apache-2.0); entry added in `e32557708`, enabled live (Ready, 5 tools, its optional folder need shown), removed in `1cbd8b60f` | Literature search is valuable, but develop now ships `literature_search` (OpenAlex, Crossref, arXiv and others) as a built-in, so an arXiv connector would duplicate it | Not shippable from here anyway: arXiv's API answered 429 or timed out from this session's shared egress on every try, so the server's search could not be qualified |

Also considered: clio-kit's HDF5 server (already a marketplace plugin, so not new to CLIO), its arXiv server (the same blocked API), Crossref and Zenodo servers on PyPI (single-author packages with one or two releases), and Hugging Face's hosted MCP (papers only behind a file-system tool).

**Logos (CN11).** PDF viewer: the server's own mark, `examples/pdf-server/pdf-icon.svg` at `ea3e9d0e624a20bc1c51310292f09d9b8ed17161`, byte for byte (sha256 `c2c19ef7…b5ab313`, the same bytes as `raw.githubusercontent.com/modelcontextprotocol/ext-apps/ea3e9d0…/examples/pdf-server/pdf-icon.svg`); terms: the repository's license (MIT, moving to Apache-2.0), no separate mark terms found; owner to confirm as for GR6. It is a black glyph, which reads at low contrast on the dark theme (`mc-o-pdf-logo-black-on-dark-zoom.png`, MC-O1). NASA Earthdata: no logo. NASA's insignia and logotype need NASA's permission (14 CFR 1221) and the server ships no mark, so the entry shows the monogram "NE".

#### Generality verdict

**Each connector itself is data only**: three YAML entries, one SVG, and a data test (`tests/test_connectors/test_grounds_entries.py::test_more_connectors_are_data_only`). No connector, offer, carrier plugin, need, reopen, look or comment code names any of them, and no server name was written into Python or TypeScript. Enabling, the per-conversation carrier (`connector-globe`, `connector-pdf`, `connector-earthdata`), the offer card (two at once, paged 1/2), View plugin, the optional value and secret rows, context, look, comments, app tools, the dashboard component and reopening all worked from the entries.

**But two MCP Apps that CLIO's earlier views did not resemble needed generic host changes**, both to match the specification's reference host (`examples/basic-host`), and running them exposed three more generic faults in how CLIO hosts a view's own calls:

| ID | Finding | Evidence | Disposition |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| MC-D1 | **Decision: a view's scripts may evaluate code** (`script-src … 'unsafe-eval'`). CesiumJS stops at load with "Unable to parse bindings … 'unsafe-eval' is not an allowed source of script" and its WebAssembly decoders with "WebAssembly.instantiate() … violates the following Content Security policy" (`mc-globe-o-before-mc-d1-cesium-blocked-unsafe-eval-desktop.png`); XA-U3 (Burette's Ketcher) is the same fault. The reference host allows `'unsafe-eval'`; the specification's restrictive default is for a view with no `ui.csp`, and "No Loosening" is about undeclared domains. CLIO's view runs on its own sandbox host, which carries no API authority, so evaluation inside it reaches nothing new; sources stay what the view declared, and a declared source still cannot carry a keyword | `gact/mcp_app_sandbox.py::_csp_header` (clio-agent `72fcecdab`); `test_mcp_app_sandbox_extraction.py::test_a_view_may_evaluate_code_but_reach_only_what_it_declared`, the conformance test's CSP line | **done; the integrator should confirm the security call** (XA-U3 left it open) |
| MC-F1 | **A tool only a view may call (`visibility: ["app"]`) was asked for by its raw id.** No agent lists such a tool, so no agent registered its title, and the permission card read "external MCP tool call: pdf_read_pdf_bytes" (also `pdf_poll_pdf_commands`, `pdf_submit_page_data`) | `sess_3a141c52a45f` allowPending log before the fix; after: "Read PDF Bytes", "Poll PDF Commands", "Submit Page Data" (`sess_0203f22e76d2`, `sess_fcf51b0309df`) | **Fixed** (clio-agent `f7ef60efd`): the gate's context for an external MCP tool carries the listed tool's own `title` (`tools/execution.py`, net one line fewer, ratchet lowered to 985); `permission_delivery.permission_summary` reads it after the registered title. Tests: `test_execution.py::test_declared_mcp_permission_gate_receives_the_tools_own_title`, `test_permission_titles.py::test_a_tool_only_a_view_calls_reads_as_its_own_title`. The transcript rows of those calls still show the raw id (MC-F3) |
| MC-F2 | **A view's own long-poll, cancelled when its page goes away or CLIO restarts, wakes the agent for an unprompted turn.** The PDF view always has `poll_pdf_commands` in flight (about 30 s each). FX-D2 cancels it when the request disconnects and records `mcp_app_call_cancelled` through `ExternalTasks._attention` (`external_task_connections.abandon_view_call`), which opens an attention episode, so `publish_task_notice` wakes the model with "task_state_uncertain". The agent then spends a turn explaining a call it never made: "A background call from the PDF view was cancelled and its outcome is unknown…" (71.4K input tokens), "The viewer has changed…" (105.6K), "The two task state uncertain notices…" (107.8K) | `sess_3a141c52a45f` (after `stack.sh restart`), `sess_0203f22e76d2` (after the harness's page closed), `sess_fcf51b0309df` (after the restart phase); `mc-o-pdf-unprompted-turn-after-view-poll-cancelled-desktop.png`, `mc-pdf-xm3-10-after-restart.png` | **Handed over** (fix stream's FX-D2 and the task ledger). Proposed fix: a view's call (`call_origin` "view") is the view's, not the agent's: record its cancellation with the typed reason and a log line, settle it terminal with `settlement: unknown`, and open no attention episode, so no model is woken. The agent learns what the view did through the view's own context, as today |
| MC-F3 | **Every result of a view's own calls is stored in full, in the transcript and in the task ledger, and a chunked-data view fills clio-core.** The PDF view reads a 4 MB paper as eight 512 KB base64 chunks on every mount (card, tab, return, reopen, dashboard). Each chunk is a `msg_view_*` row with the whole result, plus the external call's inline task value (`_events/t/content/…`). One conversation held 140 `read_pdf_bytes` results, 36.9 MB of JSON; the 128 MB store filled (`AsyncPutBlob … returned code 12`, 6,917 lines, then `clio_core_put_write_lost`), and the next turn hung on "RecordingUnconfirmed('Core observations require reconciliation')". The rows also clutter the conversation ("Called 63 tools", "Show 27 earlier", one row every 30 s while the view is open) | `sess_0203f22e76d2`; `.cloud-run/agent-run1.log` (kept until cleanup); `mc3-pdf-view-call-rows-raw-ids-before-fix-desktop.png` | **Handed over** (cross-cutting: tool observer, view-call transcript FX-D6, external task ledger). Proposed fix: for a view's call to a tool the model cannot see (`visibility` without "model"), keep the call in the trace and the activity count but store neither its result content nor a transcript row (or one folded row per view: "The view read 4.0 MB in 8 parts"); store a size and hash in the task record instead of the inline value. Until then the PDF viewer is opt-in only |
| MC-F4 | **A view set as `srcdoc` cannot draw a CesiumJS globe.** With MC-D1 the globe loaded but showed only stars: no OpenStreetMap tile was ever requested, and its context read "0.0km wide × 19980.0km tall". Bisected outside CLIO: the same view under CLIO's CSP in a plain frame draws the Earth; under a minimal fake host in a plain frame it flies to Rainier; nested CLIO's way (outer sandbox document, inner `srcdoc` frame) it draws nothing, with or without the CSP. The reference host writes the view with `document.open/write/close` and says so ("srcdoc … the CesiumJS Map won't work with") | `mc-globe-f4-before-fix-srcdoc-stars-only-desktop.png` (`sess_268fd6bca2a3`); after the fix the same conversation's Reopen draws Washington (`mc-globe-f4-after-fix-rainier-card-desktop.png`) | **Fixed** (clio-agent `f277b4db0`): the sandbox document writes the view into its frame; the frame keeps the sandbox origin and inherits the same CSP; `srcdoc` remains the fallback when the frame cannot be written. `test_mcp_apps.py` asserts the new loading; `test_mcp_app_snapshot_paint.py` (headless Chromium) still passes. Harness: view documents are found by their sandbox parent (`_lib.mjs::isViewDocument`), since they are no longer `about:srcdoc` |
| MC-F5 | **An app view inside a dashboard spills out of its cell at the docked width.** Both new apps, built into `clio.mcp-app.v1` beside a `clio.data-table.v1` by plain requests, were found by the agent's own `capture_a2ui_surface` covering the table's title, header and first row at the 500 to 644 px dock; the agent shrank the view's height and republished each time. Maximized, both dashboards lay out correctly | PDF `sess_fcf51b0309df`, globe `sess_02ad1909b859` (the agent's answers quote the captures) | **Handed over** (A2UI `clio.mcp-app.v1`: the view frame should be bounded by its cell, not by its own reported or declared height) |

#### The 3D globe through the cross-app matrix

`sess_02ad1909b859` (the headline conversation), desktop, merged code with MC-D1 and MC-F4; phone in `sess_268fd6bca2a3` ("Show me Mount Rainier on the globe."). Feature numbers as in "Cross-app matrix". Every request plain.

| Feature | Result |
| --- | --- |
| 1 card, Open in tab, Return | **pass.** Card 780 px (`mc-globe-f1-card-desktop.png`), Open in tab 668 px, one frame (`mc-globe-f1-tab-desktop.png`), Return to the card, one frame. Phone: card 311 px drawing Washington (`mc-globe-card-rainier-phone.png`), the docked sheet 337 px (`mc-globe-sheet-rainier-phone.png`, its own "Loading globe…" note over the tiles while it refits) |
| 2 maximize | **pass**: marker kept, 0 navigations; the globe fills the workbench (`mc-globe-f2-maximized-hero-desktop.png`) |
| 3 context | **pass, with an upstream gap.** The view reports its camera ("… centered on lat. / long. [45.5000, -124.0000]"); "What am I looking at?" → the agent added `look_at_view` and the app tool `get-current-view` by itself and described the coast, Alaska, the Great Lakes, and that no box is drawn. The view's sentence reads "The map view of undefined is now 0.0km wide × 19980.0km tall" when the whole globe is in view (MC-U4) and names `toolInfo.id`, which CLIO does not send (MC-O7) |
| 4 look | **pass, plain**: "Look at the view and tell me what you see." → `look_at_view`; it described the tiles, the borders and labels that had loaded since its previous look, and again that the Cascadia box is not outlined (`mc-globe-f4-look-hero-desktop.png`) |
| 5 region comment | **pass**: S1 over the Pacific Northwest (`mc-globe-f5-region-comment-desktop.png`) → "about half Pacific Ocean and half Pacific Northwest land… the southern end of Vancouver Island and the Strait of Georgia… the 49th parallel… Puget Sound…", from the crop, no tool call |
| 6 dashboard | **pass, plain, after MC-F5.** "Make a dashboard with this globe next to a table of the SWOT datasets you found." → `publish_dashboard_report` with `clio.mcp-app.v1` (`globe.show-map`, the Cascadia box) beside `clio.data-table.v1` (15 SWOT collections, version, format, DOI); "Check that the dashboard looks right." → its capture found the globe covering the table's title at the 644 px dock, it shrank the globe and republished (v2). Hero, maximized, dark and light (`mc-globe-f6-dashboard-swot-table-hero-{dark,light}-desktop.png`) |
| 7 reopen | **pass.** Close → "View closed" with Reopen (`mc-globe-f7-closed-desktop.png`), Reopen in its tab (`mc-globe-f7-reopened-hero-desktop.png`). After `stack.sh restart` (the MC-F4 restart) Reopen in `sess_268fd6bca2a3` drew Washington (`mc-globe-f4-after-fix-rainier-card-desktop.png`); in the headline conversation the harness's restart phase did not find the Reopen line and recorded "not mounted" |
| 8 theme | **N/A by the app**: the globe has no host theming (body transparent, no host tokens); its imagery reads the same on CLIO's dark and light chrome (`mc-globe-hero-{dark,light}-desktop.png`) |

## Open and upstream

| ID | Item | Why it is open | Status |

| AQ34 | "The view changes without reloading" is not met: Burette at `b1ac8ae` registers no app tools (F10), so a scene change arrives through `render_molecular_scene` as a new view in a new card. The agent's choice and Burette's actions are right. Meeting it needs Burette to register app tools (e.g. a scene-actions tool) upstream; the plan's wording is amended | upstream | partial |
| QG-B1 | **Burette's narrow-viewer crash.** When its frame matches `(max-width: 600px)` (`lib/widget.ts` 223–227) the view loads `burette-hosted-mobile.js` → `/burette-viewer/viewer.js`, which never loads `native-viewer-menus.js`, and `viewer.js:6156` `initBuretToolbar` calls `window.BuretteNativeViewerMenus.styleToolbar(...)` without the `?.` every other call site uses, so `startMolstar` throws `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'styleToolbar')` and nothing is drawn. A CLIO tab at 1440×900 is 524 px; a phone card 300 px. **The one-line fix upstream:** `window.BuretteNativeViewerMenus?.styleToolbar(...)` at `viewer.js:6156` (and `:13760`), or load `native-viewer-menus.js` (served, HTTP 200) in the mobile runtime list. Nothing in CLIO's host chooses the path: the view decides only by `matchMedia` or the user agent | upstream (Burette maintainer) | open |
| QG-B2 to QG-B4 | View-side notes, not faults: Burette's desktop shell probes `/__burette/conformer-status` and `/__burette/xtb-status` on its own origin, which on CLIO is the sandbox host and refuses them (two 403 console lines per load, correct); Mol* probes WebXR (`xr-spatial-tracking` policy lines, harmless; granting XR is not wanted); the inline card draws at the 180 px minimum because the view reports its own height (Open in tab gives the full view) | view-side | static |
| PV-N1 | **Release pin: clio-kit.** clio-agent pins `clio-kit==2.12.3`, which ships the old 26-tool paraview server, so the `paraview` connector starts the old server until clio-agent pins a clio-kit release with paraview-mcp 3.1.0 (3.0.0 was the rebuild; pv-ui bumped it to 3.1.0, PVUI-D3). Every ParaView run through CLIO in this ledger installed the stream's `ws/*` clio-kit editable into the worktree's venv. Publishing the new server to the MCP registry is also the next release's decision (PV2-D1).
